The permittee shall test the effluent for toxicity in accordance with the provisions in this\n      section.\n\n                   APPLICABLE TO FINAL OUTFALL(S) 001\n REPORTED AS FINAL OUTFALL          001\n CRITICAL DILUTION (%)              23%\n EFFLUENT DILTION SERIES (%)        10%, 13%, 17%, 23%, 31%\n TEST SPECIES AND METHODS           Daphnia pulex/ Method 2021.0 (EPA/821/R-\n                                           02-012 or latest version)\n                                    Pimephales promelas/ Method 2000.0 (EPA-\n                                           821-R-02-012 or latest version)\n SAMPLE TYPE                        Defined in PART I\n\n\n   b. The NOEC (No Observed Lethal Effect Concentration) is herein defined as the greatest\n      effluent dilution at and below which lethality that is statistically different from the control\n      (0% effluent) at the 95% confidence level does not occur. Acute test failure is defined as\n      a demonstration of a statistically significant lethal effect at test completion to a test\n      species at or below the critical dilution\n\n   c. This permit may be reopened to require WET limits, chemical specific effluent limits,\n      additional testing, and/or other appropriate actions to address toxicity.\n\n 2. REQUIRED TEST CONDITIONS AND TEST ACCEPTABILITY CRITERIA\n\n       The permittee shall repeat a test, including the control and all effluent dilutions, if the\n       procedures and quality assurance requirements defined in the test methods or in this\n       permit are not satisfied, including the following additional criteria:\n\fNPDES PERMIT No. NM0020583\n                                                                         Page 4 of PART II\n\nCondition/Criteria                        Daphnia pulex                 Pimephales promelas\n# of replicates per concentration                  4                                2\n# of organisms per replicate                       5                               10\n# or organisms per                                20                               20\n         concentration\n# of test concentrations per               5 and a control                   5 and a control\n         effluent\nHolding time *                          36 hours for first use            36 hours for first use\nTest Acceptability Criteria          ≥90% survival of all control      ≥90% survival of all control\n                                                  organisms.                       organisms.\nCoefficient of Variation **         40% or less, unless significant   40% or less unless significant\n                                            effects are exhibited.            effects are exhibited.\n\n      * If the flow from the outfall(s) being tested ceases during the collection of effluent\n      samples, the requirements for the minimum number of effluent samples and the minimum\n      number of effluent portions are waived during that sampling period. However, the\n      permittee must collect an effluent composite sample volume during the period of\n      discharge that is sufficient to complete the required toxicity tests with daily renewal of\n      effluent, and must meet the holding time between collection and first use of the sample.\n      When possible, the effluent samples used for the toxicity tests shall be collected on\n      separate days. The effluent composite sample collection duration and the static renewal\n      protocol associated with the abbreviated sample collection must be documented in the\n      full report required in Item 3 of this section.\n      **Test failure may not be construed or reported as invalid due to a coefficient of\n      variation value of greater than 40%, or a PMSD value greater than the higher value on the\n      range provided.\n\n  a. Statistical Interpretation\n      The statistical analyses used to determine if there is a significant difference between the\n      control and the critical dilution shall be in accordance with the methods for determining\n      the No Observed Effect Concentration (NOEC) as described in the appropriate method\n      manual listed in Part II or the most recent update thereof.\n\n  b. Dilution Water\n\n          1) Dilution water used in the toxicity tests will be receiving water collected as close\n             to the point of discharge as possible but unaffected by the discharge. The\n             permittee shall substitute synthetic dilution water of similar pH, hardness, and\n             alkalinity to the closest downstream perennial water for;\n                  i. toxicity tests conducted on effluent discharges to receiving water\n                     classified as intermittent streams; and\n\fNPDES PERMIT No. NM0020583\n                                                                       Page 5 of PART II\n\n                ii. toxicity tests conducted on effluent discharges where no receiving water is\n                    available due to zero flow conditions.\n\n         2) If the receiving water is unsatisfactory as a result of instream toxicity (fails to\n            fulfill the test acceptance criteria), the permittee may substitute synthetic dilution\n            water for the receiving water in all subsequent tests provided the unacceptable\n            receiving water test met the following stipulations:\n                  i. a synthetic dilution water control which fulfills the test acceptance require-\n                     ments was run concurrently with the receiving water control;\n                 ii. the test indicating receiving water toxicity has been carried out to\n                     completion,\n                iii. the permittee includes all test results indicating receiving water toxicity\n                     with the full report and information required; and\n                iv. the synthetic dilution water shall have a pH, hardness, and alkalinity\n                     similar to that of the receiving water or closest downstream perennial\n                     water not adversely affected by the discharge, provided the magnitude of\n                     these parameters will not cause toxicity in the synthetic dilution water.\n\n  c. Samples and Composites\n        1) The permittee shall collect two samples (flow-weighted composite if possible)\n           from the outfall(s).\n\n         2) The permittee shall collect a second sample (composite samples if possible) for\n            use during the 24-hour renewal of each dilution concentration for each test. The\n            permittee must collect the composite samples so that the maximum holding time\n            for any effluent sample shall not exceed 36 hours for first use of the sample. The\n            permittee must have initiated the toxicity test within 36 hours after the collection\n            of the last portion of the first composite sample. Samples shall be chilled to 6\n            degrees Centigrade during collection, shipping, and/or storage. A holding time up\n            to 72 hrs is allowed upon notification to EPA and NMED of the need for\n            additional holding time.\n\n         3) The permittee must collect the composite samples such that the effluent samples\n            are representative of the discharge duration, and of any periodic episode of\n            chlorination, biocide usage or other potentially toxic substance discharged on an\n            intermittent basis.\n\fNPDES PERMIT No. NM0020583\n                                                                       Page 6 of PART II\n\n 3. REPORTING\n\n  a. The permittee shall prepare a full report of the results of all tests conducted pursuant to\n     this part in accordance with the Report Preparation Section of the most current\n     publication of the method manual, for every valid or invalid toxicity test initiated,\n     whether carried to completion or not. The permittee shall retain each full report and\n     submit them upon the specific request of the Agency. For any test which fails, is\n     considered invalid, or which is terminated early for any reason, the full report must be\n     submitted for agency review.\n\n  b.
